The index check keeps one error per access: `IndexCheck.At` is the index expression's position, so `a[i] = a[k]` gives two errors, while the two bytes of an element and a scaled index still give one. `ErrorTests`, `IndexCheckTests`.

- **M20, index checks and signed loop variables** (2026-11-08). One commit per step.
  - *C habits* (`defb96e`, spec 13, question 75): the lexer reports `n++` / `n--` before the end of a line, `;`, a comment or a closing bracket ("Tern has no '++'. Write 'n += 1'"; the operand as written since the review fixes); `a++b` and `a--b` still parse as `a + (+b)`, `a - (-b)`. `ParsePrimary`: a `*` where a value is expected ("Tern has no '*' to read through a pointer. Write 'p[0]' for the value p points to"), `%` right before a number ("'%' does not start a binary number. Write binary numbers as 0b0000_1111"). `Parser.Expect`: a keyword where a name is expected ("Expected function name, found 'step', which is a keyword. Use another name"), also for a parameter name. `ErrorTests.Names_habits_from_c` (positions too), `The_fix_for_a_c_habit_is_the_suggestion`, `Plus_plus_and_minus_minus_between_operands_are_still_two_operators`. `errors.md` shows the new messages.
  - *`x += 2` / `x -= 2`* (`30ddfa3`, spec 11): two `inc` / `dec` on a one-byte variable in memory that is not volatile (u8 and i8, provisional: question 77). `SmallShapeTests.Adding_two_to_a_byte_in_memory_is_two_inc`. No benchmark has it; `costs.md`'s match examples shrank by 3 to 6 bytes.
  - *Signed loop variables* (`91d1b02`, spec 5.1, 11): `Checker.CheckFor` types the counter (a negative literal or a signed bound: `i8` if both fit, else `i16`; an `i8` bound with a literal outside -128..127 makes `i16`; a negative literal with an unsigned variable bound is an error that says which `as` to write; two variables of mixed sign keep the general mixing error). `CodeGen.StepSigned`: ±1 is the unsigned code, tested for equality (`inx / cpx #10 / bne`, `dex / bpl` for `n..=0`, `dex / bne` for `n..0`, the 16-bit `inc / bne / inc` and `lda / bne / dec / dec`, `lda c+1 / bpl` for a 16-bit `n..=0`); an inclusive variable end is tested before the step. A larger step with constant bounds compares with the value after the last (the values are distinct, so equality ends it); with a variable bound it leaves on V and compares against the end flipped once before the loop (`txa / clc / adc #3 / bvs / tax / eor #$80 / cmp e / bcc`; 16-bit: the add, `bvs`, a signed compare). `SignedEmptyCheck`: `sbc / bvc / eor #$80 / bpl` once before a loop with variable bounds (`SignedCounterBranch`, also 16-bit); `n..=0` / `n..0` branch on the flags of the `ldx n` (`bmi`, `beq / bmi`) when nothing was emitted between, else `cpx #0` or `lda c`. A variable step and `using` on an `i16` counter keep their existing errors. `SignedLoopTests` (86: every form at edge values against a C# model with the counter in a register and in memory, i16 runs of more than 256 passes, unnamed counters, the spec 11 shapes, the errors). `language.md` describes signed counters.
  - *Index checks* (`3c7d2fc`, spec 3.2, 5.2, 14.4): `CodeGen.Indexed` marks each indexed array operand with its table (`IndexCheck`: name, folded shift, bytes, elements, stride; the low or high table of a split array, a struct field's table, a linear array's bytes, a linear or indexed record array by record, an offset table); `MatchCheck` marks the two `lda jumphi,x` / `jumplo,x` reads of an `indexed match`. The mark is on `VarOperand` / `LabelOperand` and not part of their equality, so the optimizer and the code are unchanged. `Assembler.Checked` turns it into `PlacedInstruction.Index` (`CheckedIndex`: base, register, zero page, table start) for compiler code only, never asm. `Tools.IndexChecker.Before` reads X or Y before each marked instruction and records the first time each goes outside its table, with the line and a count. `Run.Program` fails a test on any error (`checkIndexes: false` keeps them in `IndexErrors`); all earlier tests run clean with it. `Runner` / `tern run`: the errors after the registers ("Index check (the program has no check; the emulator found these):"), `indexErrors` in `--json`, exit code 3 when main returned (provisional: question 78); an `indexed match` value outside its span stops the run (provisional: question 78). `RunOptions.MainFile` names the main file in the messages. `IndexCheckTests` (16). `SKILL.md` shows a `tern run` with an error; `language.md` says where indexes are checked.
  - Not done: `tern bench` runs with the check but does not print the errors (`RunResult.IndexErrors` has them). An `i8` loop counter in X stored to memory (`out = i`) is `txa / sta out`, where a `u8` one is `stx out` (in `SignedLoopTests`' shapes as the body).

- **M19 part 2, the Tern skill** (2026-11-08). One commit per step.

  - *The skill* (`e485191`, `ba2045a`, `.claude/skills/tern/`, provisional location: question 72): `SKILL.md` (the tools and the loop write / compile / `tern run --dump` / `tern bench`, the emulator's limits, a first program, an error in the terminal and as `--json`, a `--profile` run, the rules a model would not guess, when to use `asm`); `language.md` (the language in brief, every section with a whole program and its `tern run` result; operator precedence, question 73); `costs.md` (code shapes as listing excerpts, rough library costs, the guide's section 7 rules each with a `tern bench` comparison); `errors.md` (23 common errors with the compiler's message and the fix); `sprite.md` (a joystick-driven sprite: the logic in a module, checked from a check program with `tern run` and measured with `tern bench --fn ship.move`, the program compiled and run for a few cycles; checked in VICE: the yellow ship at 160, 140, black border, no drift with no input).

A wrong cycle count or listing line fails with the file and line (checked by hand).

- **M19 part 1, review fixes** (2026-11-08, `docs/reviews/m19.md`, part 1 section).
  - Finding 1 (must fix, `fedd660`): `CodeGen.GenInline` emits the caller's source line again after the inline body when the body changed it, so the rest of the caller's statement (`sta a,x` after `a[i] = g()`, the branch of `if cpu.carry()`) is on the caller's line in the listing and the source map. `LineTableTests.Code_after_an_inlined_call_belongs_to_the_callers_line` and `A_cpu_flag_test_is_on_the_line_that_uses_it` (`if`, `while`, `v =`; they fail without the fix).
  - Finding 2 (must fix, `68ba6fb`): `BenchVariant.Snippet` starts the source with no empty line when there is no `--decl`; `BenchVariant.Place` maps a generated line to `decl N` and its line in that declaration, or to the snippet's line (the generated `fn main() {` and `}` to its first and last lines). `BenchToolTests`.
  - Finding 3 (must fix, `adab68f`): `MemoryDump.Parse` rejects an empty name (also `:4`), a count of 0, and a variable with a count past `$FFFF` with a `FormatException`. `RunToolTests.A_bad_dump_says_what_is_wrong`.

- **M19 part 1, compiler and tools** (2026-11-08). One commit per step.
  - *`-> bool using overflow`* (`5214e10`, spec 6.1): `Register.Overflow`; `if f()` is `bvc`, `!f()` `bvs`, `v = f()` a branch over `lda #1` / `lda #0`. Allowed on an `inline asm fn`, an `asm fn` and a routine at a fixed address; a plain or inline `fn` is an error ("no instruction sets the V flag"), as a parameter or fixed input the usual errors. `OverflowTests`.
  - *`cpu` module* (`79e77d5`, `src/lib/cpu.tern`, spec 10.1): `carry()`, `zero()`, `negative()`, `overflow()` as empty-body `inline asm fn`s. `CpuModuleTests` (each flag after `lda`/`clc`/`adc` over edge values, `if`, `!`, `v =`, `while` first and later tests, the listing's one branch).
  - *`indexed match`* (`7b5dcde`, spec 5.2, 11): a plain `match` is always a compare chain; `indexed match` always a jump table with no range check (`CodeGen.GenMatch`, `MatchStmt.IsIndexed`). For an `i8` the table offset is the unbiased lowest value, so there is no `eor #$80` (`sbc #$FC` for a lowest value of -4); none for a lowest value of 0. The report lists each table ("Jump table of the indexed match at file:line:col in f: $xxxx-$xxxx, n bytes for m values", `CodeGen.MatchTables`). Errors: no pattern besides `_`; X and Y both held by `using` loops. `interp`, `variants/interp.return` and `examples/raster.tern` say `indexed match`; `interp`'s `_` arm became `HALT =>` since HALT (9) is outside the span 0..8 (provisional, below). Benchmarks: interp 242 / 5573 -> 237 / 5223. `MatchTests` (u8, i8 with a negative lowest value, with and without `_`, gaps, ranges, a masked value over all 256 bytes, a loop using X, shapes, the report, a plain match of 8+ compares stays a chain, `indexed` still a name), and the jump-table programs of `HeldVariableTests` and `DeadStoreTests`.
  - *Questions 65-67* (`3233df6`): the two-roots error for a loop counter names the loop (and a named counter) and suggests `using X` or `using Y` on it (`VariableSymbol.IsLoopCounter`); `MatchTests`' fixed-address read declares the variable `volatile`. `InterruptTests` (both messages; a shared function whose counters use Y and X runs).
  - *`tern --json`* (`d4a5b7a`, `Tools/Diagnostics.cs`): `{ ok, files, report, diagnostics: [{ file, line, column, severity, message, suggestion }] }`. The suggestion is split off the error text (provisional, below). The plain error output is unchanged. `DiagnosticTests`.
  - *Source map and `.lines`* (`19170ef`, `Tools/LineTable.cs`): `CompileResult.Instructions` holds every instruction with its address, size, text, base cycles, file, line and section. `tern` writes `<output>.lines`: per source line the bytes, the cycles of its instructions once each (`+` when a branch or page crossing adds more) and the addresses; with a run's `Profile`, how often the line's first instruction ran and the cycles it took. A loop's step and test now follow its own source line in the listing, and a function's closing `rts` its declaration line (comments only, no code change), so they are counted there. This is the source-to-instruction map M20's index check needs. `LineTableTests`.
  - *`tern run`* (`5f786f6`, `Tools/Runner.cs`, `RunReport.cs`): runs until main returns, `--cycles n` (default 20,000,000), a `brk`, an illegal opcode, or a ROM address with no stand-in. Memory is plain RAM; the screen starts as spaces; CHROUT (screen and printed text), GETIN (no key) and PLOT have stand-ins that take no cycles. `--irq n` raises IRQs through `$FFFE`. `--dump` takes a variable (`score`, `main.count`, with its value), `name:count`, `$C000-$C0FF`, `$C000:16` or decimal; `--screen` prints the screen as text; `--profile` adds each function's calls and cycles (callees included) and the `.lines` table with run counts; `--json`. Exit code 0 when main returned, 2 otherwise. `CompileResult.Variables` gives every variable's address, size, type and function. `RunToolTests`.
  - *`tern bench`* (`76bee4d`, `Tools/Bench.cs`): variants are `.tern` files or `--code` snippets (the body of main, after shared `--decl` lines; `;` separates statements); per variant the program bytes, the measured function's bytes (`main` or `--fn name`), calls, cycles and cycles per call, zero-page and other bytes of its variables and the program's globals with addresses, and `--dump` values ("The variants give different results" when they differ). Snippet errors are given at their line in the snippet. `BenchToolTests`.
  - The CLI (`src/Tern.Cli/Program.cs`) has the subcommands `run` and `bench`; `tern --help` lists every option. The tool code is in `src/Tern.Compiler/Tools/`, so the tests call it directly (the CLI only parses arguments and prints).
  - `TERN_COMPARE=1` was run (`573d811`): `comparison.md`, `variants.md` and the profiles are regenerated. Checked in VICE: `examples/raster.tern` (eight border bands, frame counter running). All examples compile (`level1`, `level2` and `player_api` are modules, not programs).

- **M18 part 3, review fixes** (2026-11-07, `docs/reviews/m18.md`, part 3 section; `7cdc594`).
  - Finding 1 (must fix): `CheckFixedAddresses` runs inside the placement loop in `TernCompiler`, so variables placed by page that overlap a fixed-address variable (or do not fit) are placed one after another instead, as on `main`; that second try reports any error. `PagePlacementTests.A_fixed_address_variable_where_the_placed_block_would_go_keeps_the_variables_together` (the review's program).
  - Suggestion 2: only indexed reads count for placement (not stores or read-modify-write instructions, which take the same cycles across a page). `PagePlacementTests.An_array_only_stored_through_an_index_is_not_placed`.
  - Suggestion 3: the retry loop's `try` / `catch` is indented with its body; the redundant second `catch` is gone.
  - No benchmark changed (1259 / 351130). 1684 compiler tests and 36 CPU tests pass.

- **M18 part 3, registers across loops and layout** (2026-11-07). One commit per step, each with its benchmark delta; measured savings in `analysis.md` section 8 ("Part 3").
  - *F7, dead stores into globals* (`6dbcfd4`, `Optimizer.RemoveDeadStores`, `OrdinaryGlobal`): F3's backward liveness also follows one-root globals of ordinary memory (not volatile, address not taken, not fixed, in a region, an overlay or data). A call, `rts`/`rti`, a jump out of the code, the end of the code, and a read through a pointer or at a number may read them (`Live.Globals`, with the bytes stored since in `Dead`). `DeadStoreTests`.
  - *F7 and F15, stores out of loops* (`99662dd`, `SinkStoresFromLoops`, `RemoveJumpsToNext`): a loop (label to a back branch) with no call, return, jump table or jump out of the code, that stores a tracked variable but never reads its memory, loses the stores; the variable is stored once at each exit from a register the analysis shows holding it (the end's exit goes after the labels only the loop jumps to, so a `break` shares it). Only when no more stores are added than removed; a global keeps its stores in a loop that reads through a pointer or at a number. A `jmp` or branch to the next label goes. `crc8`'s bit loop is the assembly's. `LoopStoreTests`.
  - *F7 and F11, a variable held in X or Y* (`da306d7`, `CodeGen.HoldInRegister`, `Served`, `ReleaseHeld`): in a `while` or `for` loop with no call, asm, `return` or loop inside (not `while true`: the store after it would never run), a `u8` one-root global or local is loaded into a free index register before the loop and stored after it (where `break` goes) if the loop assigns it. Every use must be one a register serves as well as memory (an index, a plain read, the left operand of an operator or compare with a simple right one, an assignment), and one must save more (an index, `v += 1` / `v -= 1`). X, or Y when every pointer access is `p[v]`; beside a held counter, the other register, when every index is the counter or the variable and there is no `match` or pointer access. `StoreA` writes a held variable with `tax`/`tay`; `v += 1` is `inx`/`iny`. `interp`'s `sp` is in X through the jump table; `sieve`'s `count` in Y. `HeldVariableTests`.
  - *F13, outer counters* (`b27a063`, `ChooseOuterCounterRegister`, `IndexesHeld`): the counter of a `for` loop with one level of `for` loops inside (no call, asm, `return` or `while`), every use served, goes in Y (X if a pointer access other than `p[counter]` needs Y, or an inner `using Y` loop has it); an inner `using` loop must take the other register, with every index in it already in a register. `ChooseCounterRegister` now also takes the other register beside one already held, when every index in the body is the counter or in a register (no pointer access or `match`). `OuterCounterTests`.
  - *F9, loop control* (`de347af`, `GenWhile`, `DecrementTest`, `FirstTestTrue`, `NonEmptyInside`): no entry `jmp` when the statement before the loop sets its variable to a constant that makes the first test true; `while v > 0` / `v != 0` (also `w.hi`) whose body ends with `v -= 1` (`w -= 256`) and has no `continue` tests the decrement's Z (`dec v / bne`; `dex / bne` for a held v) after one test on entry; `for i in 0..v` first in a `while v > 0` body has no empty-range test. `GenCompare`: unsigned one-byte `x > 0` is `x != 0` (no `cmp`). `bubble`'s outer loop is the assembly's. `LoopControlTests`.
  - *F12, page placement* (`0250d80`, `Allocator.Spans`, `PlaceByPage`, `RamPlan.PageOffset`, `Reserve.PageOffset`, `Assembler.NoPageStart`): zeroed and noinit global arrays the code reads through an index register (offsets collected from the code in `TernCompiler`) are placed first so that no span crosses a page (one span per table of a split or struct array, widened down to a read below the start such as `a-1,x`); the other variables fill the gaps from the top, and the block starts that far into a page instead of keeping the space before the first variable. The assembler puts the block at the first address with that low byte in the first area with room; if there is none, or a `jmp (v)` vector would then start at $xxFF, `TernCompiler` allocates again with the variables one after another, as before. The report names the arrays and their addresses; the listing notes the free bytes before the block (RAM, not in the file). `PagePlacementTests`.
  - Tests changed with the new code (each still checks its point): `WideningAddTests` shape in separate functions; `SmallShapeTests` (`c += 1` before `c = 0`; `stx x` for the held x); `DeadLoadTests` (`sta arr`); `BinaryTests` keeps (the loops' `n` is held in a register, so `uses_x`/`uses_y` keep only A); `InterruptTests`' unnamed-counter error has a call in the outer loop; `AsmTests`' vector sweep runs with arrays (placed) and with structs (the padding before the variables); `LibraryTests` cycles (`text.dec8` 63..290, `dec16` 285..1521, `mem.fill`/`copy` 14762 / 16777).
  - `TERN_COMPARE=1` was run: `comparison.md`, `variants.md`, `library.md` and the profiles are regenerated. Checked in VICE: `arcade` (score past 16 bits, bonus a multiple of 25) and `scroller` (map and 24 sprites intact); all examples compile.
  - Not done: `bubble`'s `x` stored at the top of the pass as the assembly does (3 cycles / 2 bytes); `enemies`' `sta main.x` / `main.y` (part 2's F3 note: a local declared inside the loop, needed in a free register on the bounce path; neither part 3 mechanism covers it); F10's "Y known 0" in `copy` (the entry test still reaches the end of the pages loop with Y unknown).

- **M18 part 2, review fixes** (2026-11-07, `docs/reviews/m18.md`, part 2 section).

- **M18 part 2, dataflow** (2026-11-07). One commit per finding, each with its benchmark delta; measured savings in `analysis.md` section 8 ("Part 2").
  - *F3* (`c242136`, `Optimizer.RemoveDeadStores`): backward liveness over the bytes of the function's own locals, parameters and temporaries (`OwnMemory`: not address-taken, volatile, fixed or in a region). A compiler `sta`/`stx`/`sty` to a byte no path reads before it is stored again or the function ends is removed. A `jsr` reads none of these bytes (spec 8.1); `rts` and `jmp (v)` read what the labels of a jump table read (`match` dispatches with `rts`); the end of the code and a jump out of it read nothing. Off when assembly patches code or calls its own subroutine (`SelfModifying`, `CallsOwnSubroutine`). `DeadStoreTests`. Two `OptimizerTests` register-parameter tests asserted a store that was already dead; they now check results.
  - *F3, not reached:* `enemies`' `sta main.x` / `main.y`. On the bounce path A is overwritten and `lda main.x` after the `if` reads memory. The report's "nothing reads them after F4" holds only on the other path. Removing them needs x in a free register on the bounce path (part 3's F7 mechanism).
  - *F18* (`bfa75b8`, `ReadCopiesFromSource`): for `lda E / sta v` (v a one-byte local stored only there, E ordinary memory), reads of v use E while a forward analysis (`HoldsBefore`) shows no write to E's variable, its index register, through a pointer, or a call. Where a store into E ends that while v is still needed, `ldy E` (or `ldx`) goes before the store, if the register (`RegistersLive`) and N/Z are dead, and `lda v` becomes `tya`. Only if every read is handled, bytes do not grow, and no read moved to E is in a loop the store is not in (`RepeatsWithout`). `RemoveDeadLoads` now also removes indexed loads of ordinary memory. `bubble`'s inner loop is the assembly's; `interp`'s `SWAP` also uses Y. `CopyFromSourceTests`.
  - *F16* (`93aa66c`, `CodeGen.AHolds`, `_aParameter`): at the start of a body, an unsigned one-byte `v > t` / `v <= t` with v the `using A` parameter still in A (straight-line code since entry, no write to A or v) and t needing no code is `cmp t` with two branches. The reload and the entry store then go. `calls`' `clamp` is the report's shape. `using AX` (review finding 1, `CompareInAX`): an unsigned 16-bit compare of the parameter, held in A and X, against an operand needing no code is `cpx hi / bcc / bne / cmp lo / ...` (equality `cmp lo / bne / cpx hi / beq`), leaving A and X unchanged. Signed compares still store the parameter (`math.abs16` in Tern). `AParameterTests`.
  - *F17* (`de90ff2`, `ForgetWrite`'s `offset`): an absolute write forgets only that byte, so A still holds `v` after `bcc / inc v+1`. `ByteWriteTests`; `WideningAddTests`' shape is shorter.
  - *F5* (`0075978`, `Element` in the register state): after `lda e,x` / `sta e,x` the register holds the element until the index register or the array changes or a call; volatile and fixed arrays are not remembered. No benchmark change; `enemies.inplace` 139 / 58,445 -> 127 / 45,709. `ElementValueTests`.
  - `TERN_COMPARE=1` was run: `comparison.md`, `variants.md` and the profiles are regenerated. `bubble.direct` and `bubble.minus` are no longer faster than `bubble`, and `enemies.inplace` is no longer worse than the locals form.
  - Checked in VICE: `arcade` (score past 16 bits, bonus a multiple of 25) and `scroller` (map and 24 sprites intact); all examples compile.

- **M18 part 1, review fixes** (2026-11-07, `docs/reviews/m18.md`).
  - Finding 1 (must fix, `aebe282`): a 16-bit element whose index reads volatile memory is no longer used in place (`IndexedOperand(e, 2)`, `ReadsVolatile`), since the in-place shapes load the index once per byte. `IndexedOperandTests.A_volatile_index_of_a_16_bit_element_is_read_once` covers the four shapes and fails without the fix.
  - Finding 2 (must fix, `f1a44fc`): `DeadLoadTests` runs `Optimizer.Run` on hand-written code for each rule of `RemoveDeadLoads` (overwritten loads, each return register at `rts`, fixed and volatile loads, branch and `jsr`, a load read through an index), plus one program.
  - Suggestion 4 (`0702476`): `GroupConstantStores` keeps each source line in front of its own statement's code (listing only).
  - Suggestion 5 (`94a30e6`): `CounterMax` uses `Checker.LargestLoopValue` for constant bounds (`0..=250 step 7` ends at 245).
  - Suggestion 3 is question 66 below; suggestion 6 (pre-existing) is under "Known limitations".

- **M18 part 1, local code shapes** (2026-11-07). Each fix has its benchmark delta in its commit message and its measured saving next to the estimate in `analysis.md` section 8.
  - *F2* (`CodeGen.FoldIndexOffset`, `_counterMax`, `CounterMax`): `a[i + c]` / `a[c + i]` is `lda a+c,x` when `i` is the counter of an enclosing `for` whose header proves `i + c <= 255`: a constant end counting up (inclusive or not), a constant start counting down, or an excluded `u8` variable end (`i <= 254`). From the header only (spec 3.2). `IndexOffsetTests`.
  - *F1* (`IndexedOperand`, `Binary16InPlace`, `InPlace16`): an element whose index loads without changing A, or a byte `p[i]` through a zero-page pointer with such an index, is the operand of `adc`/`sbc`/`and`/`ora`/`eor`/`cmp` itself: 8-bit operators (either side for commutative ones), 8-bit compares (not with the left in a register: `cpx`/`cpy` have no indexed form), 16-bit compares on either side, and 16-bit `+ - & | ^` computed byte by byte into the destination, also straight into an array element (`fib[i] = fib[i - 1] + fib[i - 2]`). An operand whose index reads the destination is not used in place. It looks through a cast between types of the same size. `IndexedOperandTests`.
  - *F4* (`GenCompare`): a one-byte compare with a constant has the constant on the right, and `x > c` / `x <= c` compare with `c + 1` (not for `c` = the type's maximum). `ConstantCompareTests` (every value, edge constants, signed and unsigned).
  - *F6* (`AddsByteTo`): `w += b as u16` (or a `u8`) is `lda b / clc / adc w / sta w / bcc / inc w+1`; `w -= b` with `b` needing no code is `lda w / sec / sbc b / sta w / bcs / dec w+1`. A constant addend keeps `clc / lda w / adc #c`. `WideningAddTests`.
  - *F8* (`ShiftedOperand16`): `x op (y >> k)` (or `<<`) with simple operands keeps the shifted low byte in A and only the high byte in a one-byte temporary. Not for a signed `>>`. `ShiftedOperandTests`.
  - *F19* (`Optimizer.UseOperandInA`): `lda x / [clc] / op y` with A already holding `y` is `op x` for `and`, `ora`, `eor`, `adc`. Tests at the end of `OptimizerTests` (class `OptimizerTests`).
  - *F21* (`CompareIntoCarry`): `return l op r` in a function returning `bool` in the carry, for an unsigned one-byte `<`, `<=`, `>`, `>=`, sets the carry without branches (`lda l / cmp r`; `lda l / clc / sbc r` for `>`; `cmp #c+1` for a constant; `cpx`/`cpy` for a register parameter). `CarryReturnTests`.
  - *F10*, all in `SmallShapeTests`: `w += 256` / `w -= 256` as `inc w+1` / `dec w+1` (other multiples of 256 add to the high byte); `a[i] += 1` as `inc a,x` (X only); negation as `lda #0 / sec / sbc m`; no `cmp #0` for a zero byte in 16-bit `==`/`!=`; no `clc` when the carry is known clear (`MaxOf`, `CarryClearAfter`: an `asl` that shifts out a 0, an `adc` that cannot pass 255, from constants and the same counter proof as F2); `n.lo` of a local or parameter as a `for` bound in place. Optimizer: a branch on Z tells the analysis the register N/Z came from is 0 on the path where Z is set (`IsZero`); a load whose value another register holds becomes a transfer, `ldy #c` becomes `iny`/`dey` (`Shorter`); stores of 0 at the start of `main` into globals the startup cleared go (`RemoveStoresOfStartupZeros`, run after the other passes, then they run again); dead loads go (`RemoveDeadLoads`; `rts` reads only the return registers; a fixed address is never removed); runs of `lda #c / sta` store each constant once (`GroupConstantStores`).
  - Not reached: F10's "Y already 0" in `copy` (the `ldy #0` after the pages loop stays: the `while` can be skipped on entry, so Y is not known there; F9 would make it so).
  - Side effects recorded in tests: `math.mul16`'s `beq` now crosses a page (cycle range 38..870), `text.dec16` 297..1641 -> 293..1637, `mem.fill`/`mem.copy` 14783 / 16798, the struct shape `enemies[i].hp += 1` is `ldx i / inc enemies+40,x`, the index-register test counts `ldx` or `tax`.
  - `TERN_COMPARE=1` was run: `comparison.md`, `variants.md`, `library.md` and the profiles are regenerated. `fib16` and `sum16` are below the assembly. `bubble.minus` is slower than `bubble` now, so `analysis.md` drops it as advice. `bubble.direct` (149,404) is faster than `bubble` (169,732) until F18.
  - Checked in VICE: `arcade` (score past 16 bits, bonus counting) and `scroller` (map and 24 sprites intact); all examples compile.

The bit test in `mul` is class 1 (F20, 50 cycles). New variant `calls.bigger` (only `bigger` inline): 103 / 29,045, which shows inlining removes the `jsr`/`rts` (which the assembly pays too), not the counted items; `calls`' class 2 cell is now `bigger`'s `using` parameters, measured by `calls.regs` (1,200), and the inverted-carry result (903) needs F21 (a `bool` returned as the compare's carry, without branches) plus a rewrite. - **M17 rework** (2026-11-07, review findings 1-9).
  - `analysis.md` rewritten: every cell counted from the profiles and listings, instruction by instruction against each `.asm`; cycle and byte tables with columns 1, 2 now (measured), 2 after a class 1 fix, 3, 4, "asm's choices" (the assembly's absolute addresses, its own page crossings, zeroed memory) and "other / not explained", which is 0 in every row. (Totals as revised: see the entry above.)

- **M17, gap analysis** (2026-11-07).

Its startup runs in the emulator with `$FFD2` as `rts`; bytes are the sections of the program and of any library routine it calls, without startup, `exit` and `__memset`. `comparison.md` regenerated (it was stale: `bubble` was 87 bytes before M15's index change): Tern 1221 / 486,802, assembly 947 / 324,419, llvm-mos 1359 / 493,092.
